            -         "MorphoAdapter: not param"
            -     );
            -     _;
            - }
            + modifier onlyParam() {
            +     _onlyParam();
            +     _;
            + }
            + 
            + function _onlyParam() internal {
            +     require(
            +         hasRole(PARAM_ROLE, msg.sender) || hasRole(DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE, msg.sender),
            +         "MorphoAdapter: not param"
            +     );
            + }
            
            
    = help: https://book.getfoundry.sh/reference/forge/forge-lint#unwrapped-modifier-logic
